# Generation and the download buttons: the packs drawn, the files named,
# the status lines, and every option handed to the exporter.
#
# Recorded from the old app at commit d81f583, the last commit before it
# was deleted. Every one of these was asserted equal to the port's at that
# point, by tests/generate-parity.test.ts and tests/export-run.test.ts.
#
# Not regenerable: the implementation that produced them is gone.
#
# Amended by hand since recording, which is the only way this file may change:
#   * 9 November 2026 — `opts.noFoils = false` added to every options dump.
#     A new option, and false is the recorded behaviour, so no pack, file or
#     status line below moved. Checked against the diff: the line was the
#     whole of it.
